Method: 提出CosmicFish-HRM，核心是分层推理模块（HRM），该模块在推理时动态分配计算资源：模型通过高低两个层次的推理循环迭代，根据输入复杂度学习何时停止。模型还集成了Grouped Query Attention、RoPE和SwiGLU等现代Transformer组件。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀推理行为，针对不同任务和输入分配不同的推理步数。虽然小规模下推理基础设施的开销相对较大，但随着模型规模增长，HRM核心的相对成本会降低，这种权衡变得更加有利。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能为提升推理能力提供一种有前景的替代方案，而非仅仅依赖参数规模的扩展。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [198] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经标度律中的指数α并非固定常数，而是依赖于优化器选择：预条件优化器（如自然梯度）能产生更陡峭的标度（更大α），在随机特征回归框架下，自然梯度的α（~0.31）是梯度下降（~0.12）的2.6倍。该效应在谱指数s≈1.0（自然语言特征）时最显著，但能否迁移到大规模LLM训练仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战标度律中α为固定常数的传统假设，探究优化器对学习标度指数的影响，从而更准确地预测模型性能随规模增长的规律。

Method: 在随机特征回归框架下，控制实验条件（5种优化器变体、6种谱条件），测量不同优化器下的标度指数α，并分析预条件优化器的影响与谱条件的依赖关系。

Result: 预条件优化器一致产生更大的α（更陡峭标度），在s≈1.0时自然梯度α≈0.31 vs 梯度下降α≈0.12（2.6倍差异）。该效应在大部分谱范围内显著，在s≈1.5处达到峰值，在s=2.0处仍保持较大。

Conclusion: 标度律预测应纳入优化器选择因素；该发现提供了预测先进优化器何时有效的谱诊断方法；但该优势能否扩展到大规模LLM训练中仍需验证。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>


### [199] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了log-alignment ratio (LAR)作为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠度量，并证明其能追踪记忆与泛化之间的转换，在grokking中预测有效维度，在3B参数语言模型预训练中跟踪泛化差距，且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化理论中引入的log-alignment ratio (LAR)原本用于衡量参数-激活对齐程度，但其性质和实际意义尚未被充分理解。作者希望将其重新形式化，并探究其能否作为泛化行为的预测指标。

Method: 将LAR重新定义为权重谱p（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱q（输入在奇异方向上的归一化平方投影分布）之间的重叠度量。通过分析p和q在训练过程中的变化来追踪记忆-泛化转换。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR能预测学习函数的有效维度：k ≈ n^{2(1-LAR)}；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR与泛化差距的偏离程度可追踪泛化差距，且其下降速率在过拟合接近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一种轻量级、无需验证数据的泛化行为指标，可以用于监控模型的过拟合程度，在深度学习训练中有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [200] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在标准图假设下，马尔可夫边界是使目标变量条件独立于其他变量的最小特征集。但在SCM3K基准（3450个任务、6种回归器）上测试发现：用真实边界显著提升预测，尤其在特征空间大且稀疏时；但通过因果发现恢复边界的标准流程效果不佳，现有估计器在边界最有用的高维区域前就已耗尽计算预算，且即使运行也难超越全特征集。原因有三：发现算法优化结构恢复而非预测、假阳假阴预测成本不对称、真实边界只是众多有效特征集之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究马尔可夫边界在表格数据预测中的实际效用，检验理论优越性是否能在实践中兑现，揭示因果发现与预测任务之间的alignment gap。

Method: 在SCM3K基准（3450个合成SCM任务，特征数40-1000，6种SCM族）上，使用6种回归器（线性回归、随机森林、XGBoost、MLP等），对比三种设置：全特征集、真实马尔可夫边界、因果发现恢复的边界。

Result: 真实边界显著提升预测性能，尤其在特征空间大且稀疏时；但因果发现恢复的边界无法超越全特征集；现有因果发现算法在特征数>200时即耗尽计算预算；假阴性比假阳性对预测伤害更大；存在许多非马尔可夫边界的特征集也能超越全特征集。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界的预测价值依赖于能否准确获取，而当前因果发现方法无法支撑该管线；需要发展面向预测对齐的特征选择方法和能利用因果结构的表格模型。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [201] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）在InceptionTime架构的EEG抑郁症检测模型中的应用，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）产生部分一致的归因模式，但方法论假设差异导致解释结果存在变异性，强调了事后可解释性在精神科EEG分类器中的价值与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基于EEG的抑郁症检测中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，缺乏对多种事后可解释性方法的系统性比较研究。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-MDD分类，在受试者层级分层5折交叉验证框架下，使用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）进行全局归因聚合分析。

Result: 不同可解释性方法产生部分收敛的归因模式，额叶、颞叶和后部EEG区域（特别是右半球）被反复强调；梯度法与扰动法之间具有实质性一致性，而DeepSHAP产生明显不同的归因分布；方法间变异性反映了方法论假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同事后可解释性方法捕获了EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关性结构，归因模式与既往EEG抑郁症研究大体一致，但分析应视为探索性而非确凿的神经生理生物标志物或临床适用性证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [202] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Curve方法，通过分段贝塞尔曲线参数化在LoRA空间中连接独立微调的最优点，形成连续低损谷，从而在保持性能的同时提升预测分布的互信息，实现功能多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调场景下，现有离散多模态方法（如深度集成）相比单模态方法优势有限，这与传统深度学习中集成独立最优点可提升泛化性能、通过连续低损谷连接模态可增强贝叶斯模型平均的观察相矛盾。需要研究LoRA空间中是否存在此类连续结构及其能否带来局部或离散方法所缺失的功能多样性。

Method: 提出LoRA-Curve，采用分段贝塞尔曲线参数化LoRA空间，包含两种变体：自由配置（联合优化所有控制点）和锚定配置（连接独立微调的LoRA最优点）。证明沿曲线的路径连续性和损失Lipschitz正则性，结合平坦最小值扰动和Jensen-Shannon散度正则化器。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 7B的推理和分类基准上，线性插值存在损失障碍，而锚定多段曲线能够通过连续低损谷连接独立最优点。LoRA-Curve在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提高预测分布的互信息，建立了连续参数空间遍历与功能多样性之间的联系。

Conclusion: LoRA-Curve通过连续的参数空间路径实现了LoRA微调中的功能多样性，弥合了离散集成方法与单模态方法之间的差距，为LoRA场景下的不确定性估计和贝叶斯模型平均提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [203] [The Hamilton-Jacobi Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28983)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Erika Fille T. Legara,Christopher P. Monterola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了神经网络训练等价于求解Hamilton-Jacobi型偏微分方程的初值问题，将网络架构（残差网络、Transformer、RNN/LSTM/SSM）统一为不同哈密顿量和粘性系数的HJ方程离散化形式，通过单个变形参数ε连接了网络理论、热带代数、粘性PDE和凸优化四个视角，并推导出泛化界、对抗鲁棒性、反向传播最优控制、标度律及影响函数等定量结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立神经网络与偏微分方程（特别是Hamilton-Jacobi方程）之间的严格数学对应关系，从而将深度学习的经验现象（如泛化、鲁棒性、标度律）纳入PDE理论框架进行统一解释和定量分析。

Method: 将每个梯度步映射为粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程的初值选择问题（Hopf-Cole传播子拟合观测），识别log-sum-exp层为精确对应，残差网络、Transformer、RNN/LSTM/SSM为结构对应（架构决定哈密顿量和粘性系数）；引入变形参数ε构建包含网络、热带代数、粘性PDE、凸优化四个视角的可交换图。

Result: 1) 固定t时最小最大最优泛化率O(n^{-1/(d+2)})；2) 对抗鲁棒性由ε控制；3) 反向传播等价于哈密顿系统的协态方程（Pontryagin最大值原理）；4) 通过PDE求积得到与数据本征维数一致的标度指数；5) 闭式O(N)影响函数（softmax归因权重π_j），其熵景观随ε增大经历折叠分岔，归因盆地合并。

Conclusion: 深度学习（训练+推理）可完全纳入粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程的PDE框架，该统一视角不仅揭示了各主流架构的深层数学结构，还导出了一系列可验证的定量预测（泛化界、鲁棒性、标度律、归因动力学），为理论理解神经网络提供了PDE工具集。

Abstract: In this paper, training a neural network is identified, exactly, as a search through Hamilton--Jacobi initial-value problems: each gradient step selects the initial data of a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation whose Hopf--Cole propagator best fits the observations; at inference, the input is the spatial point at which that solution is evaluated and the initial condition is already encoded in the weights. The correspondence is exact for log-sum-exp layers and structural for broader architectures: residual networks, transformers, and recurrent architectures (RNNs, LSTMs, SSMs) each discretize the same class of Hamilton--Jacobi equations, with architecture-dependent Hamiltonian and viscosity. A single deformation parameter $\varepsilon$ unifies all four perspectives (network, tropical algebra, viscous PDE, convex optimization) in a commutative diagram closed under Lipschitz conditions. Quantitative consequences include: the minimax optimal generalization rate $O(n^{-1/(d+2)})$ for fixed $t$; adversarial robustness controlled by $\varepsilon$; backpropagation as the co-state equation of the Hamiltonian system for residual networks (Pontryagin Maximum Principle); scaling exponents consistent with data intrinsic dimension via PDE quadrature; and a closed-form $O(N)$ influence function (softmax attribution weights $π_j$) whose entropy landscape undergoes fold bifurcations as $\varepsilon$ increases, each merging attribution basins.

</details>


### [204] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机i.i.d.情境下的上下文赌博机问题，聚焦于奖励向量满足$s$-稀疏性的设置（即每个上下文的奖励$L_1$范数不超过$s \ll |A|$）。主要贡献是设计了两种算法，能以高概率输出$ε$-最优策略，样本复杂度为$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$，并将结果推广到一般Natarano类，同时给出匹配的下界（对数因子内），显著改进了先前工作中$Θ(|A|^9)$的额外依赖。第一种方法基于决策-估计系数（DEC）的分析框架设计探索-优化算法；第二种方法基于低方差探索技术，得到具体可实现的算法，并扩展到上下文组合半赌博机场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文赌博机研究（特别是与多类别分类相关的稀疏奖励场景）存在显著的样本复杂度差距——先前工作（Erez et al., 2024, 2025）引入了$Θ(|A|^9)$的额外依赖，本文旨在填补这一理论空白，获得最优的样本复杂度上界。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1) 将上下文赌博机问题重构为结构化观测下的上下文决策制定问题，利用决策-估计系数（DEC）框架设计探索-优化算法，证明稀疏奖励下模型类的DEC边界与$s$成比例，直接导出最优速率；(2) 基于低方差探索技术设计更具体、可实现的算法，并自然扩展到上下文组合半赌博机（如多类别列表分类）。

Result: 1) 提出样本复杂度为$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$的算法（以高概率输出$ε$-最优策略）；2) 将结果推广到一般Natarano类；3) 给出匹配的下界（对数因子内），消除先前工作的$Θ(|A|^9)$额外依赖；4) 扩展到上下文组合半赌博机，改进了带通配符的多类别列表分类的样本复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上闭环了稀疏奖励上下文赌博机问题的样本复杂度（最优率），并通过DEC框架和低方差探索两种路径分别提供了信息论层面的理论理解和实际可实现的算法，同时为更广泛的组合半赌博机场景提供了改进保证。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [205] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，利用正样本对从高维、小样本的fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且可泛化的特征，在多个下游任务中优于全监督基线，接近大规模模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据采集成本高、精神科量表主观性强，导致数据集样本量小且标签质量不稳定，加之fMRI数据的高维特性，模型过拟合风险高。现有fMRI基础模型预训练和微调的计算资源要求过高。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，仅使用正样本对（positive-only data pairs）进行训练，无需负样本或大量计算资源。

Result: 学习到的表示在多个下游分类和回归任务中表现优异，超越全监督基线，性能接近大规模模型。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam展示了在数据有限的神经影像应用中自监督学习的潜力，为小样本fMRI分析提供了高效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [206] [Kernel Renormalization in Bayesian Deep Neural Networks: the Equivalent Wishart Ansatz in the Proportional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29684)
*Paolo Baglioni,Christian Keup,Vincenzo Zimbardo,Rosalba Pacelli,Alessandro Vezzani,Raffaella Burioni,Pietro Rotondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有效的近似方法，用于预测固定深度L的贝叶斯多层感知机（MLP）在任意高维数据上的泛化性能。通过提出等价Wishart假设来捕捉MLP层次经验核的主要随机涨落，并在比例极限下对配分函数进行大偏差分析，将强表示学习编码为最多L个标量序参数。该方法还扩展到了卷积架构（CNN），识别了层次局部核重正化机制。在L~O(10)和P~O(10^3)的深度神经网络上进行了实验验证，结果与理论高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层（单隐藏层）神经网络在比例宽度极限下的非微扰结果已被深入研究，但将这些结果扩展到深层非线性网络极具挑战性。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提出一种有效的近似方法，用于预测深层贝叶斯MLP在比例极限下的泛化性能。

Method: 提出等价Wishart假设，捕捉MLP层次经验核的主要随机涨落；在比例极限下对配分函数进行大偏差分析，用最多L个标量序参数自洽地编码强表示学习；将方法扩展到CNN，识别层次局部核重正化机制。

Result: 在L~O(10)和P~O(10^3)的深度神经网络上，针对经典基准数据集的贝叶斯后验采样实验，整体上理论与实验高度吻合，同时发现两种类型的系统偏差。

Conclusion: 该有效理论能够很好地预测深层贝叶斯神经网络在比例宽度极限下的泛化性能，为理解深度神经网络的表示学习机制提供了理论工具。

Abstract: The scaling limit where both the size of the training set $P$ and the width $N$ of a deep neural network grow at the same rate, the so-called proportional-width regime, has been intensely studied for shallow, single-hidden-layer networks. However, extending these non-perturbative results from shallow architectures to deep non-linear networks has proven very challenging. Here we present an effective approximate approach to predict the generalization performance of Bayesian multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) of fixed depth $L$ on arbitrary high-dimensional data. We propose an equivalent Wishart Ansatz to capture the dominant stochastic fluctuations of the hierarchical empirical kernels of MLPs. This allows us to perform a large deviation analysis for the partition function of MLPs in the proportional limit, expressed in terms of a renormalized NNGP kernel. In this description, even strong representation learning in the proportional limit is encoded in at most $L$ scalar order parameters, determined self-consistently. Extending the approach to convolutional architectures (CNNs), we identify a hierarchical local kernel renormalization mechanism, which allows to quantify more complex data-dependent transformations of the large-width kernel in CNNs due to finite-width effects. We test our effective theory against sampling experiments from the Bayesian posterior of finite deep neural networks with depths $L \sim O(10)$ and $P\sim O(10^3)$ on classic benchmark datasets, finding overall very good agreement together with two distinct types of systematic deviations.

</details>


### [207] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对MathCheck (ICLR 2025) 的释义质量审计发现3.1%的语义错误，移除后GPT-4o排名从第2降至第4；跨模型一致性检测可自动发现这些错误（成本<$10）。进一步发现Claude Haiku 4.5准确率86%但SCR仅50%，而9个模型的准确率范围86-96%但SCR范围50-82%，存在32个百分点的评估鸿沟。形式化证明了"无免费基准"推论，并提出FormInv审计协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试中释义（paraphrase）可能存在语义错误，导致模型评估排名不可靠。同时，标准准确率指标无法反映模型在语义等价重述下回答的一致性，存在被忽视的评估鸿沟。

Method: 对MathCheck进行释义质量审计，使用跨模型一致性检测（≥3/4或≥6/9模型一致）自动识别语义错误；提出语义一致性率（SCR）和每定理Cochran's Q作为不变性度量；设计FormInv审计协议和FormInvSelector模型选择工具。

Result: MathCheck中3.1%的释义语义错误，修正后GPT-4o排名下降；9个模型准确率范围仅86-96%，但SCR范围达50-82%，存在32个百分点鸿沟；形式化证明了"无免费基准"推论——任何目标排名均可通过加权释义族实现。

Conclusion: 标准基准测试的准确率指标掩盖了模型在语义等价条件下的不一致性，释义族的选择隐式决定了模型排名，需引入语义一致性等不变性度量进行更可靠的评估。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [208] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CB-SLICE方法，利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的概念预测失败来识别错误切片，相比现有方法提供更忠实、更丰富的模型错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释与模型推理过程脱节，仅近似误差源而不够准确，需要一种更忠实于模型内部机制的误差切片识别方法。

Method: 利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中下游任务失败通常源于概念预测失败这一特性，提出CB-SLICE方法，将共享概念预测失败的样本分组，并识别每个切片失败模式中最关键的关键词概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CB-SLICE在发现已知偏见方面优于最先进的方法，并提供更丰富、更忠实的模型错误解释。

Conclusion: 基于概念的误差切片发现方法能提供更准确、可解释的错误分析，有助于模型调试和偏见缓解。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [209] [FedQHD: Closed-Form Function-Space Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29002)
*Yuchen Hou,Yongshan Chen,Zhuowen Zou,Calvin Yeung,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedQHD，一种使用超维（随机特征）状态编码器和线性读出层的联邦Q学习方法，通过函数空间一致聚合解决FedAvg参数平均在异构网络下的不一致问题，并在连续状态控制任务上取得优于或媲美基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedAvg风格的参数平均在联邦强化学习中存在函数空间不一致问题：当客户端使用异构编码器或相同非线性网络时，平均参数并不对应客户端值函数的加权平均，导致聚合效果不佳。

Method: 使用超维（随机特征）状态编码器结合线性读出层，使Q函数在状态上非线性但在可训练参数上线性。共享编码器时直接加权平均本地读出矩阵；异构编码器时服务器利用共享锚点状态集平均客户端Q值构建全局教师，每个客户端通过一次岭回归投影将教师编译到本地表示中。

Result: 在四个连续状态离散动作控制基准上，FedQHD匹配或优于FedAvg风格基线及蒸馏替代方法，同时计算量显著降低；联邦间隙对编码器维度的经验依赖性与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: FedQHD通过线性结构实现函数空间一致的联邦聚合，并理论刻画了异构编码器下的联邦间隙来源（子空间错位、锚点集条件数、正则化偏差），为联邦强化学习提供了高效且理论可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning enables decentralized agents to collaboratively improve policies or value estimates without exchanging raw trajectories. However, FedAvg-style parameter averaging is not function-space consistent: when clients use heterogeneous encoders or even identical nonlinear networks, averaged parameters need not correspond to the weighted average of client value functions in any common function space. We propose FedQHD, a federated Q-learning method using hyperdimensional (random-feature) state encoders with a linear readout, so that Q-functions are nonlinear in state yet linear in trainable parameters. This linear structure enables closed-form aggregation. With a shared encoder, the function-space consensus update coincides exactly with weighted averaging of local readout matrices. With heterogeneous encoders, the server constructs a global teacher by averaging client Q-values on a shared anchor-state set, and each client compiles this teacher into its local representation via a single ridge projection. We formalize the federation gap -- the error incurred when compiling a federated teacher into a heterogeneous client representation -- relative to a client-specific oracle projection. We show that this gap decomposes into subspace misalignment, anchor-set conditioning, and regularization bias. We further identify the anchor-to-dimension ratio $m \geq D_i$ as the well-conditioned regime in which the gap reduces to a multiple of the encoder heterogeneity floor. On four continuous-state, discrete-action control benchmarks, FedQHD matches or outperforms FedAvg-style baselines and distillation-based alternatives while requiring substantially less computation, and the empirical dependence of the federation gap on encoder dimension matches our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [210] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRe，一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的包装器，通过每步动态预算限制仅评估高冲突/高不确定性的交互，在最大独立集（MIS）和旅行商问题（TSP）上实现8-15倍加速、12-44倍内存降低，且保持解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器在每次迭代中反复评估密集的边/因子交互，导致推理耗时长且受内存瓶颈限制，难以扩展到大尺度问题。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法启发，LoRe在推理时每步仅评估固定比例的交互，通过动态路由计算到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，而非使用静态的kNN图或固定掩码进行稀疏化。

Result: 在MIS问题上，推理规模扩展超过基线3倍，实现约8倍加速和约12倍峰值内存降低，解质量保持。在TSP上，n=1000时实现约15倍加速和44倍内存降低，零样本泛化到拓扑偏移，路径质量有竞争力。

Conclusion: LoRe是一种训练无关、即插即用的推理加速方法，通过动态交互预算分配显著提升扩散求解器的可扩展性，在多个组合优化任务上验证了有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [211] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机重设（非平衡统计物理）与岭回归（统计学习）之间的理论联系。在线性梯度流中，以速率r重设至原点产生的稳态均值恰好为岭估计量λ=r。对于指数重设时间分布，该恒等式在每个特征方向上精确成立；非指数重设则生成替代谱滤波器。在含噪声的OU扩展中，仅在均值层面成立，协方差结构不同。实验展示了非指数重设滤波器与岭回归的预测差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非平衡统计物理中的随机重设机制与统计学习中的岭正则化建立理论联系，揭示两者在数学结构上的深层对应关系，并探索非指数重设律能否产生超越传统岭回归的谱滤波器。

Method: 利用拉普拉斯变换关系连接岭回归与梯度流的指数时间平均，将指数时间解释为泊松重设的稳态年龄。从线性梯度流出发推导重设稳态均值为岭估计量；扩展到一般更新重设律，分析指数分布的唯一性；再引入OU噪声作为SGD近似，研究波动层面的协方差与风险。

Result: 指数重设时间分布是唯一能在每个正曲率特征方向上精确恢复岭回归标量滤波器的更新律；非指数重设生成替代谱滤波器。在含噪声扩展中，均值等式成立但协方差非零，与确定性岭估计不同。实验展示了非指数重设滤波器的预测差异。

Conclusion: 随机重设与岭回归在均值层面存在精确等价关系，且指数分布具有唯一性；非指数重设可生成更丰富的谱滤波器家族，为设计替代正则化方案提供了新视角。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [212] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种基于因果智能的通用框架，通过学习上下文特定的因果图与结构因果模型，结合约束感知的多目标优化，为系统状态转换设计最优干预策略，并在合成与真实生物数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型大多缺乏机制性洞察，且没有用于决策的因果推理框架，难以通过定向干预实现系统状态转换。

Method: COAST首先学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型，将观测到的分布偏移归因于机制层面的因果驱动因素，然后提出一种约束感知的多目标优化方法，平衡转换效能、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性。该框架是模块化、领域无关的，整合了特征选择、因果发现、因果建模、干预识别与评估等可互换组件。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据集上，COAST成功恢复了关键因果驱动因素，识别出稳健的单靶点和多靶点干预策略，实现了期望的状态转换，并提供了透明的机制性解释以指导实验验证。

Conclusion: COAST为状态转换问题提供了一种因果驱动的、可解释的干预设计框架，具有跨领域应用的潜力。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [213] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模型熵（CME）作为无标签强化学习奖励信号，利用独立验证器模型对生成器响应的平均对数似然作为奖励，在开放指令遵循任务上超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练受限于奖励信号：基于真实可验证奖励的方法仅适用于数学或代码等自动正确性检查领域；人工偏好标签成本高且易遭奖励破解；无标签方法依赖自指信号（如多数投票、token熵）会强化模型自身错误。

Method: 1. 定义CME为生成器响应在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然；2. CME无需训练、连续、不依赖自一致性；3. 将CME集成到GRPO框架中，不改变训练循环其他部分。

Result: 在UltraFeedback提示词上，基于AlpacaEval 2.0评估，CME奖励在四种模型家族（Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo）和三种训练阶段（预训练、SFT、指令微调）的头对头LLM-as-Judge对比中均超越未训练基线，调整平局后的胜率范围为52.5%至71.4%。

Conclusion: CME扩展了无标签强化学习到开放指令遵循领域，解决了自指信号无法应用或表现不佳的问题。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [214] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出关系型任务外推器（RTE），通过将目标任务分解为已知锚任务和变换关系来学习任务间的变换规律，从而实现对未见任务的系统外推，在函数预测和序列预测任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统在插值上表现出色，但难以泛化到训练分布支持范围之外的未见任务，即便在简单场景（如处理超出训练范围的任务参数）和基础模型中也存在此问题。

Method: RTE将目标任务分解为一个已知锚任务和连接锚任务与目标任务的变换关系，然后学习一个关系运算符，将锚-变换对映射到目标任务的预测结果。

Result: 在参数外推、长度外推和组合外推等多种任务外推场景中，RTE在函数预测和序列预测任务上均大幅优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 外推本质上是关系性的，通过学习任务间的变换关系可以有效实现系统外推。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Designing Active Tether-Net Systems for Space Debris Capture with Graph-Learning-Aided Mixed-Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29021)
*Feng Liu,Achira Boonrath,Gishnu Madhu,Eleonora M. Botta,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对主动系绳网系统（用于捕获空间碎片）的设计与控制联合优化问题，提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的优化方法。该方法将混合组合非线性规划（MCNLP）问题简化为普通非线性规划（NLP），利用GNN对候选组合进行评分推荐，结合粒子群优化（PSO）与梯度微调求解。实验表明，相比直接求解MCNLP，GNN方法能更快收敛到近似最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动系绳网系统是捕获空间碎片等大型非合作目标的有效方案，但其设计与控制涉及连续变量、整数变量（网连接性）和类别变量（组件选择）混合的复杂非线性优化问题（MCNLP），现有二进制编码方法对高度非线性、多模态的工程MCNLP效果不佳，整数编码又可能引入虚假关联，因此需要更高效的优化方法。

Method: 利用组合空间的图结构特性，训练图神经网络（GNN）以候选设计的连续变量向量为输入，输出对各候选组合（表示为图中节点）的评分，据此推荐最优组合。从而将MCNLP优化问题降维为普通NLP问题，再使用粒子群优化（PSO）加梯度微调方法求解。

Result: 在系绳网形态、MU质量和推进器选择、控制器瞄准点的联合设计问题上，基于GNN推荐的方法相比直接求解MCNLP，能够显著更快地收敛到相似的优化解。

Conclusion: 所提GNN辅助优化方法有效解决了系绳网系统设计与控制的MCNLP问题，在收敛速度上具有明显优势，且该方法与底层NLP求解器无关，具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: Active tether-net systems are a promising solution for capturing large non-cooperative targets, such as space debris, by deploying a flexible net manipulated by maneuverable units (MUs). However, concurrent systematic explorations of design and control choices of the tether-net system to understand its full potential remain limited, partly due to the complex, constrained, nonlinear optimization problem that it presents -- one that involves a mixture of continuous, integer and categorical variables, with the latter two arising from net connectivity and component choices, respectively. Classical binary encoding methods are often ineffective for solving highly nonlinear and multimodal Mixed Combinatorial Nonlinear Programmings (MCNLPs) in engineering design, while integer coding approaches can introduce spurious relations among combinations. Given the graph-structured characteristics of the combinatorial space, this paper adopts and extends a new graph-learning-aided optimization approach to solve this MCNLP problem. Here, a Graph Neural Network (GNN) is trained to score (as output) and thereof recommend candidate combinations represented as nodes in a graph, with the continuous variable vector portion of a candidate design given as input. As a result, the MCNLP optimization reduces to an NLP, which can be solved using standard solvers. While this reduction approach is agnostic to the choice of the NLP solver, here a state-of-the-art Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithm with gradient-based fine-tuning is used as the solver. Demonstrated on the problem of concurrently designing the morphology of the net, choice of mass and thrusters in the MUs and aiming points used by the controller of the tether-net system, the GNN-based recommender is shown to provide significantly faster convergence to similar optimal solutions, compared to direct solution of the MCNLP problem.

</details>


### [216] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今为止最大规模的post-hoc校准基准评测（近2000个实验），涵盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务，提出以PHI（评分规则改进）作为校准评估的替代指标，并通过系统实验得出：平滑校准函数优于分箱方法、高维场景需专用多分类方法、通用机器学习模型缺乏竞争力等关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有post-hoc校准方法众多，但缺乏大规模、标准化的统一评测，导致难以确定哪些方法在实践中真正有效。

Method: 构建覆盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务的大型基准，包含近2000个实验，集成多种经典模型、深度学习架构和基础模型的预测结果，统一实现数十种校准方法；提出PHI（Post-Hoc Improvement）作为校准评估的替代指标。

Result: 平滑校准函数优于分箱方法；高维场景下专用多分类方法至关重要；通用机器学习模型缺乏校准特定设计时竞争力不足。

Conclusion: 通过大规模系统实验揭示了post-hoc校准方法在不同场景下的适用规律，并开源了完整的基准平台以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [217] [Return-to-Go Is More Than a Number: Q-Guided Alignment for Return-Conditioned Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29028)
*Yuxiao Yang,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-ALIGN DT框架，通过确保策略的Q值与输入RTG一致来对齐条件序列模型中的控制信号，理论证明能学习近优策略，实验在D4RL基准上取得更优的可控性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件序列模型将RTG视为简单数值输入，未将其与策略的实际性能对齐，导致控制信号与策略输出之间的不一致。

Method: 利用Q函数为CSM提供密集指导，并通过RTG扰动技术微调Q函数，确保更高RTG映射到更高期望回报的轨迹。

Result: 在D4RL基准上取得更优的可控性和性能，有效学习结构化策略族，在速度跟踪任务上优于先前方法。

Conclusion: Q-ALIGN DT通过RTG与Q值对齐，实现了精确可控的策略学习，具有理论保证和实证优势。

Abstract: Conditioned Sequence Models (CSMs) learn policies by treating return-to-go (RTG) as a control signal. However, existing CSMs often treat the RTGs as simple numerical inputs rather than aligning them with the performance of their policies. In this paper, we propose Q-ALIGN DT, a framework that enforces this alignment by ensuring the $Q$-value of the output policy is consistent with the input RTG. By leveraging a $Q$ function to provide dense guidance to CSMs and further fine-tuning it using an RTG-perturbation technique with the CSM, our method ensures that higher RTGs are consistently mapped to trajectories with higher expected returns. Theoretically, we show that Q-ALIGN DT can efficiently learn the desired policy and output a near-optimal one when the RTG is sufficiently high. Empirically, we demonstrate through extensive experiments that Q-ALIGN DT achieves superior controllability and performance across the D4RL benchmark. Remarkably, our model effectively learns a structured family of policies that maintains precise alignment and generalizes to tasks like velocity-tracking where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [218] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符和CCA对齐方法，在异构数值表格数据集间进行可解释的跨数据集相似度度量与变量级对应关系恢复的框架，在15个数据集上取得了0.9的P@1分数，并支持差分隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型缺乏原生机制来跨异构特征空间有意义地表示数值数据集；现有方法要么需要共享变量定义（针对单一数据集的预测建模），要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐机制。

Method: 通过结构化探索性数据分析（EDA）描述符表征数值表格数据集，利用预训练句子变换器将描述符嵌入共享向量空间，采用典型相关分析（CCA）量化跨数据集相似度，并引入带惩罚项的CCA恢复稀疏、可解释的变量级对应关系，同时可选应用差分隐私保护描述符。

Result: 在15个数据集（涵盖通用基准、材料信息学和核级石墨表征）上评估，总P@1分数达到0.9，最近邻检索和聚类结构在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该框架为将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成（RAG）流水线中提供了一条保留统计上下文的原则性路径，可直接应用于数据驱动的算法选择和未知数据集的仿真模型初始化。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [219] [Reasoning with Sampling: Cutting at Decision Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30327)
*Felix Zhou,Anay Mehrotra,Quanquan C. Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings算法，利用基础模型的下一个词元熵值作为代理来识别推理轨迹中的关键决策点，并从中重采样，从而高效地从幂分布中采样，在多个基准上优于基线和RL训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明从幂分布采样可激发基础模型的推理能力，但高效采样需要混合到幂分布，而先前方法使用均匀随机剪切位置往往重写局部细节而非重新审视决策点，导致采样效率低。

Method: 提出Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings算法，利用基础模型的下一个词元熵值作为代理识别关键决策点（如证明策略或算法的选择），从这些熵值较高的位置进行重采样。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA Diamond和AIME26基准上，该方法一致优于基线方法和RL训练模型。在简化的推理模型中证明，该方法的混合时间与轨迹中的决策数量成比例，而非与词元数量成比例。

Conclusion: 熵值跳跃是识别推理决策点的有效代理，基于熵的剪切策略比均匀随机剪切更高效，可实现无需额外训练的推理能力提升。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning models are produced by posttraining base language models with reinforcement learning. Recent work has challenged this by showing that sampling from a sharpened version of the base model's distribution, a so-called power distribution, elicits comparable reasoning without additional training, curated datasets, or verifiers. However, making this method practical requires efficiently sampling from the power distribution. A sampler needs to "mix" to the power distribution, which necessitates moving between modes of the target distribution; intuitively, e.g., trying different reasoning strategies. The samplers proposed in prior works repeatedly select a "cut" position in the current reasoning trace uniformly at random and resample the suffix from that position onward. However, reasoning traces typically contain a few consequential decisions (e.g., the choice of proof strategy or algorithm), and we observe that a uniformly chosen cut tends to rewrite local details rather than revisit decision points. We introduce an algorithm (Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings) that uses the base model's next-token entropy as a proxy to identify key decision points and resample from those positions. We empirically verify that entropy jumps are a useful proxy for decision points and, in a stylized model of reasoning, prove that our method's mixing time scales with the number of decisions in a trace rather than with the number of tokens, which can be much larger. Across MATH500, HumanEval, GPQA Diamond, and AIME26, our method consistently improves over baselines and RL-trained models.

</details>


### [220] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL)框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，以加速基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中的推理过程，在D4RL任务中实现更高效的计算性能，并在离线到在线RL任务中展现出更快的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中推理延迟过长，导致迭代采样成为计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出MoMa QL框架，利用统计假设检验中的最大均值差异（MMD）技术，匹配原始分布与目标分布之间的所有阶统计量，对条件分数函数施加强矩统计正则化以保证分布级收敛。

Result: 在D4RL任务中计算效率更高且性能可比甚至更具竞争力；在离线到在线RL任务中，由于加速了基于流策略的动作采样过程，展现出更快的适应性和更强的性能。

Conclusion: MoMa QL通过MMD矩匹配有效加速生成模型在RL中的推理，在不同超参数下保持稳定，并在离线到在线迁移中表现优越。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [221] [Parallel Adaptive Multi-Objective Evolutionary Learning of Discretized Bayesian Network Classifiers for Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29058)
*Damy M. F. Ha,Tanja Alderliesten,Peter A. N. Bosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯网络学习算法Baymex存在的计算效率低和仅评估于合成数据的问题，提出了并行化策略和自适应防过拟合机制，并将其重构为分类器。在16核CPU上实现最高54倍加速，并在三个真实临床数据集上取得了与经典模型相当或更优的预测性能，同时生成紧凑、可解释的贝叶斯网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baymex算法虽然在学习离散化贝叶斯网络上优于现有方法，但存在两大不足：1) 计算时间开销大；2) 仅在合成数据上进行了评估，缺乏真实场景验证。本文旨在提升其可扩展性并验证其在真实临床分类任务中的有效性。

Method: 1) 引入并行化策略提升计算效率；2) 设计自适应引导机制以减少过拟合；3) 重构Baymex用于分类任务，通过多目标优化交叉熵损失和BIC复杂度项来训练贝叶斯网络分类器；4) 在两个开源数据集(RADCURE, SUPPORT)和一个内部临床数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在16核CPU上实现最高54倍以上的加速比；在三个临床数据集上与决策树、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯和随机森林等基线相比，Baymex获得了统计上相似或更优的预测性能；生成的贝叶斯网络紧凑且临床可审查，包含与已知临床因素一致的预测因子。

Conclusion: 提出的改进使Baymex在真实临床分类任务中兼具高效性和可解释性，能够产出与临床知识一致的紧凑贝叶斯网络，是一种有前景的临床决策支持工具。

Abstract: Bayesian Networks (BNs) are of interest from an explainable AI viewpoint, offering transparent probabilistic models for decision support. Baymex is a recently introduced multi-objective evolutionary algorithm for learning discretized BNs, enabling experts to trade-off different objectives of interest, such as likelihood, model complexity, and prior beliefs. While Baymex has been shown to outperform state-of-the-art BN learning approaches, Baymex still 1) requires a lot of computation time and 2) has only been evaluated on synthetic data. To improve scalability, we introduce a parallelization strategy as well as a mechanism that enables adaptively steering optimization toward networks that overfit less. We furthermore reconfigure Baymex to train a BN classifier through multi-objective optimization of cross-entropy loss and the BIC complexity term so as to evaluate its performance on real-world clinical classification tasks. Besides observing speedups up to over 54 times on a 16-core CPU, comparisons against clinically familiar baselines (decision trees, logistic regression, naive Bayes, and random forests) on two open-source (RADCURE and SUPPORT) and one in-house dataset, show that Baymex obtains statistically similar or better predictive performance while producing compact, clinically inspectable BNs. Importantly, Baymex finds multiple plausible BN classifiers that contain predictors consistent with established clinical factors.

</details>


### [222] [Ensemble Score Filtering for Real-Data Energy Consumption Forecast Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29072)
*Ruoyu Hu,Dahai Yu,Feng Bao,Guang Wang,Guannan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用集成评分滤波器（EnSF）对高维实际能耗数据进行数据同化，以改进基于预训练黑箱时空预测模型的状态估计。EnSF通过基于分数的扩散模型和闭式分数表示近似滤波分布，避免重训练神经网络。实验表明，EnSF在长期预报修正和非线性观测条件下优于开环传播和集成卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际能源消耗数据常存在部分、噪声或延迟观测，这促使研究者利用学习型预测模型进行状态预测，并结合数据同化方法进行序贯预报修正。

Method: 采用预训练的黑箱时空预测模型作为状态传播器，利用集成评分滤波器（EnSF）同化部分和带噪声的观测数据，通过基于分数的扩散模型和闭式分数表示（配合蒙特卡洛近似）近似滤波分布，无需在数据同化过程中重新训练神经网络评分模型。

Result: 开环传播的预测模型在长时域上不可靠，EnSF校正能够显著改善状态估计；在非线性观测设置下，EnSF的校正效果优于集成卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）。

Conclusion: EnSF作为一种无需重训练的数据同化方法，能有效结合预训练预测模型与部分观测数据，在能耗状态估计中表现优于传统方法，尤其适用于非线性观测场景。

Abstract: Accurate estimation and forecasting of energy consumption are important for power-system operation, planning, and demand-side management. In practice, however, complete and timely measurements may not always be available, and the observed data can be partial, noisy, or delayed. This motivates the use of learned forecasting models for predicting the evolving consumption state, together with data assimilation methods for sequential forecast correction. In this work, we study a high-dimensional data assimilation problem for real energy-consumption data. \modeltext{The forward prediction is supplied by a pretrained black-box spatio-temporal forecasting model, which is treated as the state propagator in the filtering procedure.} We employ the Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) to assimilate partial and noisy observations and to correct the forecast trajectory over time. The EnSF uses score-based diffusion models to approximate filtering distributions and avoids retraining neural-network score models during assimilation by using a closed-form score representation and Monte Carlo approximation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that open-loop propagation of the learned forecasting model can become unreliable over long horizons, while EnSF-based correction substantially improves state estimation. Comparisons with the Ensemble Kalman Filter (EnKF) further show that EnSF provides stronger correction under the nonlinear observation setting considered in this work.

</details>


### [223] [Knowledge Offloading: Decomposing LLMs into Sparse Backbones and Memory Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29075)
*Karim Galliamov,Rochelle Choenni,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识卸载(KOFF)框架，将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享主干和领域专用外部记忆模块，在约12%全局稀疏度下保持模型性能，实现知识在共享核心与可切换外部记忆间的重新分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM将通用能力和领域知识编码在同一参数集中，缺乏模块化组织。本文探究能否将专业知识从共享参数中分离出来，存储在外部记忆模块中，实现知识结构的重组。

Method: 基于冻结的基础模型，联合学习结构化剪枝掩码和轻量级恢复模块（LoRA适配器+学习型键值缓存），将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享主干和领域专用记忆。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen系列3B-8B模型上，约12%全局稀疏度下KOFF保持大部分未剪枝模型性能，而同等剪枝但无记忆模块的基线模型性能急剧下降。LoRA和KV记忆互补，语言专用神经元优先被移除，语言通用神经元保留在主干中。

Conclusion: 知识可以在共享核心与可切换外部记忆之间进行有意义的重新分配，这为LLM的模块化设计和知识管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLMs encode both general capabilities and domain-specific knowledge in a single set of parameters. We ask whether this capacity can be reorganized: keeping broadly useful computation in a shared backbone, while moving specialized knowledge into external memory modules. We propose \emph{knowledge offloading} (KOFF), a framework for decomposing a pretrained LLM into a sparse shared backbone and domain-specific memories. Starting from a frozen base model, we jointly learn a structured pruning mask and lightweight recovery modules, implemented as LoRA adapters and learned key-value caches. Across Llama and Qwen models from 3B to 8B, we find that non-trivial capacity can be moved out of the shared backbone without a large loss in model ability. At around 12\% global sparsity, KOFF preserves much of the unpruned model's performance, while pruning the same frozen model without memories degrades sharply. Ablations show that LoRA and learned KV memories are complementary, and specialization analyses suggest that the learned decomposition is meaningful: language-specific neurons are preferentially removed while language-general neurons largely remain in the backbone. These results suggest that knowledge can be reallocated between a shared core and swappable external memories.

</details>


### [224] [OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089)
*Xinyu Liu,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Yang Zhou,Jindong Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OISD框架，通过在策略内部自蒸馏机制，将最终层的预测信号迁移到中间层，结合logit对齐和注意力对齐，显著提升数学推理任务的强化学习后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练方法主要使用稀疏的最终胜败奖励来优化输出策略，忽略了中间表示中编码的预测信号，导致推理能力提升受限。

Method: 提出OISD框架，在GRPO优化过程中，将最终层同时作为策略和分离的内部教师，通过两种互补机制指导选定的中间层：logit对齐（传递高层推理行为）和注意力对齐（保持一致的注意力模式），并使用带符号优势权重的Jensen-Shannon对齐损失进行蒸馏。

Result: 在四个数学推理任务上，OISD相比于强推理RL基线取得了持续且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 通过将最终层的预测信号蒸馏到中间层，可以有效增强强化学习后训练中的推理能力，且不需要外部特权信息。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD

</details>


### [225] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型合并形式化为一个关于残差更新的凸二次规划问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出最小化平方输出校准目标，并推导出预测合并质量的封闭形式诊断指标。实验表明该方法在单层和多层设置下均优于或匹配现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如task arithmetic、model soups、TIES、DARE）计算高效且经验成功，但依赖启发式设计选择，缺乏形式化的最优性保证。本文旨在为模型合并提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 将模型合并建模为关于残差更新的凸二次规划（QP），以平方输出校准为目标函数，利用校准输入和微调模型输出求解最优权重。该方法统一了现有方法作为特例，并推导出残差能量捕获比例的封闭形式诊断指标。扩展到多层面合时采用顺序逐层算法。

Result: 单层设置下QP匹配或优于现有方法；当最优基比廉价的对角QP有显著增益时，给出了特征刻画；在语言和视觉基准上，逐层多层面合方法取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 模型合并的凸二次规划形式化框架具有理论最优性保证，统一了现有启发式方法，并提供可预测合并质量的诊断工具，在实际多任务场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [226] [Bridging Chemists and AI: An Expert-Augmented Framework for Interpretable Route Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29108)
*Yujia Guo,Mikhail Kabeshov,Tat Hong Duong Le,Samuel Genheden,Marco V. Mijangos,Varvara Voinarvoska,Giulia Bergonzini,Ola Engkvist,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专家增强的数据驱动评分框架，结合机器学习与化学领域知识，用于有机合成路线的多目标评估。基于DeepSets模型，利用树编辑距离训练并微调专家评估数据，输出定量分数和可解释的分类（Good/Plausible/Bad），在类别预测和评分排名上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的合成路线评估系统过于简化多目标性质，依赖专利路线等代理数据集，缺乏普遍适用的评估标准，亟需一个结合专家知识、可解释且准确的路线评估方法。

Method: 提出专家增强的数据驱动评分框架：使用DeepSets模型，以参考路线与机器生成路线之间的树编辑距离为训练目标，再通过专家评估结果进行微调，输出定量评分及Good、Plausible、Bad三类可解释分类。

Result: 类别评估预测的Spearman相关系数为0.78，Pearson相关系数为0.77；评分预测的Top-1准确率为60.2%，远超基线方法的17.5%。

Conclusion: 结合领域知识的深度学习框架能有效提升合成路线评估的准确性和可解释性，为药物化学和工艺化学中的路线选择提供可靠支持。

Abstract: Selecting efficient multi-step synthetic routes is a central challenge in organic synthesis, particularly in medicinal and process chemistry, where route choice directly impacts feasibility, cost, and development efficiency. Data-driven assessment systems often oversimplify the multi-objective nature of synthesis design and rely on proxy datasets, such as patent routes, rather than universally grounded criteria. To address this, we introduce an expert-augmented, data-driven scoring framework that integrates machine learning with chemists' domain knowledge for both numerical and explainable route assessment. A DeepSets-based model is trained using tree edit distance between reference and machine-generated routes, and then fine-tuned with expert evaluations to produce both quantitative scores and interpretable qualitative categories: Good, Plausible, and Bad. The resulting system achieves a Spearman correlation coefficient of 0.78 and a Pearson correlation of 0.77 for category assessment prediction, and 60.2% top-1 ranking accuracy for score prediction, substantially outperforming the previous baseline of 17.5%.

</details>


### [227] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针可以近乎完美地解码模型表示，但该表示方向可能与模型实际使用的计算方向正交（即不相关）。本文通过日历日期推理任务证明，sin/cos探针从层激活中恢复的"一年中的第几天"方向与模型实际因果计算子空间正交，而分布式对齐搜索（DAS）找到的四维子空间才是模型真正依赖的计算路径。这种"读出-中介夹角"与随机子空间夹角无显著差异，表明探针学到的是与模型计算无关的方向。电路逆向工程揭示注意力头和MLP通过特定偏移机制完成日期到时长的转换。稀疏自编码器分解证实探针对齐和DAS对齐的特征编码了语义不重叠的概念。该现象在1.5B-9B四个规模、两个模型家族上复现，并在空间位移和符号算术任务上获得初步证据，表明该问题具有普遍性，直接质疑了基于探针的运行时安全监控方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证线性探针解码的表示方向是否与模型实际使用的因果计算方向一致，揭示探针可解性≠因果相关性这一根本问题，并评估其对可解释性安全应用的威胁。

Method: 使用sin/cos探针和分布式对齐搜索（DAS）分别提取表示方向，通过"读出-中介夹角"（与Haar均匀随机子空间基线比较）量化方向正交性，结合电路逆向工程定位注意力头QK偏移机制（±30天和±61天）及MLP转换功能，并用稀疏自编码器分解验证特征语义分离。

Result: 探针方向与模型因果子空间正交（夹角与随机子空间无差异），消融探针方向不影响模型性能而消融DAS子空间完全破坏性能；正交性在1.5B-9B四个规模、两个模型家族上复现，并在空间位移和符号算术任务上获得初步证据。

Conclusion: 探针可解性与因果相关性之间不存在必然联系，读出-中介正交性是探针可解释性的一种普遍失效模式，直接否定基于探针的运行时安全监控方案的可靠性。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [228] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展模型家族至新尺寸和硬件格式的经济高效方法，基于Apertus 8B LLM，通过蒸馏生产了Apertus-v1.1系列模型（最大4B参数，1.7T令牌训练），在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面展现了成本效益和强大精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于各种场景（如聊天助手、数据标注），需要满足不同的预算和硬件约束。当前LLMs以批次形式发布不同尺寸的相似模型以适配广泛约束，但需要一种经济高效的方法来扩展模型家族至新的尺寸和硬件格式。

Method: 采用蒸馏和量化作为核心方法，基于开源配方的Apertus 8B LLM，蒸馏出参数规模达4B的模型家族（Apertus-v1.1），使用1.7T许可令牌进行训练。

Result: 该方法在覆盖大范围的硬件和系统需求方面展现出成本效益和强大的精度性能。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化可作为成本有效的方式将模型家族扩展到新的尺寸和硬件格式，满足多样化的硬件约束与应用需求。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [229] [Unveiling Multi-regime Patterns in SciML: Distinct Failure Modes and Regime-specific Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29153)
*Yuxin Wang,Yuanzhe Hu,Xiaokun Zhong,Xiaopeng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Tianyu Pang,Michael W. Mahoney,Yujun Yan,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了科学机器学习（SciML）模型在不同超参数设置下呈现的多“机制”（regime）行为，提出了一种机制感知的诊断框架，通过联合分析性能、训练动态和损失景观几何，发现了三个关键结论：一致的三机制结构、优化的机制特异性以及精细故障模式对传统损失景观指标的挑战，并在PINN、神经算子和神经ODE等模型上验证了这些发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示SciML模型中因超参数差异导致的多机制行为，并提出一种统一的、任务无关的诊断框架，以理解不同机制下模型的故障模式，从而指导模型鲁棒性的提升。

Method: 提出机制感知的诊断框架，联合分析模型性能、训练动态和损失景观几何特征；在多种SciML模型（PINN、神经算子、神经ODE）上，结合不同约束施加方式和优化器设计进行系统性实验验证。

Result: (i) 多种标准SciML模型中一致出现三机制结构；(ii) 不同优化方法在不同机制下表现各异，没有在所有机制中均有效的方法；(iii) SciML模型存在精细故障模式，可能挑战传统损失景观指标的解释效力。

Conclusion: 该工作为建立统一的、任务无关的SciML故障模式分析视角提供了可行路径，并可为机制感知的鲁棒性提升策略提供指导。

Abstract: Neural networks trained under different hyperparameter settings can fall into distinct training "regimes," with consistent behavior within regimes and qualitative differences across regimes. In this paper, we study such multi-regime behavior in scientific machine learning (SciML) models through a regime-aware diagnostic framework that jointly analyzes performance, training dynamics, and loss-landscape geometry. We identify three key findings: (i) a consistent three-regime structure emerges across many standard SciML models, different constraint enforcements, and various optimizer designs; (ii) optimization effectiveness is regime-specific, with no single method performing well across all regimes; and (iii) SciML models can exhibit fine-grained failure modes that can challenge conventional interpretations of standard loss-landscape metrics. Our results provide an approach to establish a unified, task-oblivious perspective on failure modes in SciML and to inform regime-aware guidance for improving robustness. We validate these findings across widely-used SciML models, including physics-informed neural networks, neural operators, and neural ordinary differential equations, on benchmarks spanning representative ordinary and partial differential equations.

</details>


### [230] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RUBRIC-ARROW框架，通过交替训练评分标准生成器和基于评分标准的评判器，结合概率评分规则和交替GRPO方案，仅使用成对偏好数据训练逐点评估器，在奖励建模准确性和下游策略训练上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐点奖励建模在主观、不可验证场景下难以进行绝对评分，现有基于评分标准的方法依赖前沿LLM且存在硬布尔聚合导致的平局问题。

Method: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW交替框架：联合训练评分标准生成器和评分标准条件评判器；RL阶段仅使用成对偏好数据；引入概率评分规则减少平局；设计阶段特定偏好奖励；采用交替GRPO方案。

Result: 在奖励建模准确性上达到有竞争力的表现，并为下游策略后训练带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW通过交替训练和概率评分有效解决了逐点奖励建模在主观场景下的绝对评分难题，提升了奖励建模和策略训练效果。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [231] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种参数化的局部线性注意力机制，将softmax注意力的局部常数估计升级为局部线性估计，通过引入可学习的查询投影器来探测KV协方差，并设计了硬件感知算法提升计算强度，在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现了帕累托改进，首次证明了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力机制的局部常数估计存在偏差-方差权衡次优的问题，而先前提出的局部线性注意力（LLA）虽理论上更优但因计算和数值稳定性问题无法在大规模LLM预训练中扩展。

Method: Parallax通过以下方式实现：(1) 消除LLA中的数值求解器，引入可学习的类查询投影器来探测KV协方差；(2) 通过带宽、投影器构建和仿射结构将Parallax置于统一注意力家族框架中；(3) 设计硬件感知算法，提升算术强度使注意力转向更计算密集的计算模式。

Result: (1) 解码核在多种批量大小和上下文长度下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3；(2) 在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中持续获得困惑度改进，并迁移到下游基准任务；(3) 在参数匹配和计算匹配控制下均保持优势，实现帕累托改进；(4) 发现Muon优化器能解锁Parallax的能力，首次展示架构-优化器协同设计。

Conclusion: Parallax成功将局部线性注意力扩展到LLM预训练中，通过架构-优化器协同设计实现了帕累托改进，为注意力机制研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [232] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol利用ColBERT风格的后期交互机制，通过残基级嵌入比较而非单向量池化，在蛋白质远程同源检索（SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准）中超越了序列比对、池化PLM等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质同源搜索在"twilight zone"（全局序列相似性弱）时，传统比对方法失去敏感性；现有基于PLM的检索方法将表示池化为单向量，可能丢失揭示远程同源性的局部基序、结构域或保守残基信息。

Method: ProtoCol将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入集合，独立编码蛋白质，保持候选表示可预计算，使用ColBERT风格的MaxSim后期交互机制对残基嵌入进行逐对最大相似度评分，实现候选排序。

Result: 在SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准测试中，ProtoCol优于序列组成方法、基于比对的传统方法、池化PLM基线以及训练的单一向量基线。

Conclusion: 残基级比较的后期交互机制是远程同源搜索的有效检索层，验证了细粒度嵌入匹配相比单向量池化在捕捉远程同源性信号上的优势。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>
